    CHECK(fabsf(hm_sample(&m, 1.25f, 0.75f, &gx, &gy) - (1 * 0.75f * 0.25f + 2 * 0.25f * 0.25f + 4 * 0.75f * 0.75f + 6 * 0.25f * 0.75f)) < 1e-5f);
    CHECK(fabsf(gx - ((2 - 1) * 0.25f + (6 - 4) * 0.75f)) < 1e-5f);
    CHECK(fabsf(gy - ((4 - 1) * 0.75f + (6 - 2) * 0.25f)) < 1e-5f);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_sample(&m, 1.9f, 1.5f, NULL, NULL) - (4 * 0.1f * 0.5f + 6 * 0.9f * 0.5f + 2 * 0.1f * 0.5f + 9 * 0.9f * 0.5f)) < 1e-5f);
    gx = 123;
    hm_sample(&m, 0.2f, 0.2f, &gx, NULL);
    CHECK(fabsf(gx - (1 * 0.8f + 1 * 0.2f)) < 1e-5f);
    hm_free(&m);

    /* plane z = 0.3x - 0.2y: exact gradient everywhere */
    Heightmap p = hm_new(10, 7);
    for (int y = 0; y < 7; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 10; x++) p.z[y * 10 + x] = 0.3f * x - 0.2f * y;
    float h = hm_sample(&p, 6.37f, 2.81f, &gx, &gy);
    CHECK(fabsf(h - (0.3f * 6.37f - 0.2f * 2.81f)) < 1e-5f);
    CHECK(fabsf(gx - 0.3f) < 1e-5f && fabsf(gy + 0.2f) < 1e-5f);
    hm_free(&p);

    /* gradient matches a finite difference of the height on noise terrain */
    Heightmap t = hm_new(40, 40);
    hm_fbm(&t, 9, 0.2f, 4);
    int ok = 1;
    for (int k = 0; k < 50; k++) {
        float x = 1 + hash01(k, 0, 1) * 37, y = 1 + hash01(k, 1, 1) * 37;
        if (x - (int)x < 0.01f || x - (int)x > 0.99f || y - (int)y < 0.01f || y - (int)y > 0.99f) continue;
        float e = 0.002f;
        hm_sample(&t, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        float fdx = (hm_sample(&t, x + e, y, NULL, NULL) - hm_sample(&t, x - e, y, NULL, NULL)) / (2 * e);
        float fdy = (hm_sample(&t, x, y + e, NULL, NULL) - hm_sample(&t, x, y - e, NULL, NULL)) / (2 * e);
        if (fabsf(fdx - gx) > 2e-3f || fabsf(fdy - gy) > 2e-3f) ok = 0;
    }
    CHECK(ok);
    hm_free(&t);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_droplet
title: Terrain 6: one erosion droplet
continues: hm_sample
--- prompt
Simulate one water droplet of hydraulic erosion (Beyer 2015, as in Lague's *Coding Adventure*). The parameters:

```c
typedef struct {
    float inertia;       /* 0.05: how much of the old direction is kept */
    float capacity;      /* 4:    sediment capacity factor */
    float min_capacity;  /* 0.01: capacity floor, so flat ground still erodes a little */
    float deposition;    /* 0.3:  fraction of surplus sediment dropped per step */
    float erosion;       /* 0.3:  fraction of spare capacity eroded per step */
    float evaporation;   /* 0.01: fraction of water lost per step */
    float gravity;       /* 4 */
    int max_steps;       /* 30 */
} ErosionParams;
```

`ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void)` returns the values in the comments. `float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y)` runs one droplet starting at `(x, y)` and returns the sediment it still carries at the end. If the start is outside `0 <= x < w - 1`, `0 <= y < h - 1`, return 0 and change nothing. Otherwise start with direction `(0, 0)`, `speed = 1`, `water = 1`, `sediment = 0`, and repeat at most `max_steps` times:

1. `ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y`, offsets `fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy`; `height, gx, gy = hm_sample(x, y)`.
2. `dx = dx * inertia - gx * (1 - inertia)`, same for `dy`. If the length is 0, stop; otherwise normalize.
3. Move: `x += dx, y += dy`. If the new position is outside `[0, w - 1) × [0, h - 1)`, stop.
4. `dh = hm_sample(new x, new y) - height`; `cap = max(-dh * speed * water * capacity, min_capacity)`.
5. If `sediment > cap` or `dh > 0`: deposit `dh > 0 ? min(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * deposition`; subtract it from `sediment` and **add** it to the 4 corners of the **old** cell `(ix, iy)` with bilinear weights `(1-fx)(1-fy)`, `fx(1-fy)`, `(1-fx)fy`, `fx fy`.
   Otherwise erode `min((cap - sediment) * erosion, -dh)`: **subtract** it from the same 4 corners with the same weights, and add it to `sediment`.
6. `speed = sqrtf(max(0, speed * speed - dh * gravity))`, `water *= 1 - evaporation`.

(Lague's published code has `+ dh * gravity`, which slows droplets down going downhill; the sign here is the physically sensible one.)

Write the bilinear deposit as a helper, `void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount)`; you will reuse it. Order of operations within a formula does not matter, the tests allow for float rounding.

--- stubs
typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    (void)m; (void)ix; (void)iy; (void)fx; (void)fy; (void)amount;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    (void)m; (void)p; (void)x; (void)y;
    return 0;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

int main(void) {
    /* 4000 droplets from a grid of start points; the difference image shows where they carved (dark) and filled (light) */
    Heightmap m = hm_new(128, 128);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 40, 6);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    float *before = malloc(sizeof(float) * 128 * 128);
    memcpy(before, m.z, sizeof(float) * 128 * 128);
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    for (int i = 0; i < 4000; i++) erode_droplet(&m, &p, hash01(i, 0, 1) * 127, hash01(i, 1, 1) * 127);
    printf("P2\n128 128\n255\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < 128 * 128; i++)
        printf("%d%c", (int)clampf(128 + (m.z[i] - before[i]) * 400, 0, 255), i % 128 == 127 ? '\n' : ' ');
    free(before);
    hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
Keep `ix, iy, fx, fy` from *before* the move: deposits and erosion go to the cell the droplet just left.

--- hint
Write the loop body in exactly the order of the numbered list; most bugs here are using the new position's cell for the deposit, or forgetting to stop before sampling outside the map.

--- harness
static void ref_add(float *z, int w, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float a) {
    z[iy * w + ix] += a * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy); z[iy * w + ix + 1] += a * fx * (1 - fy);
    z[(iy + 1) * w + ix] += a * (1 - fx) * fy; z[(iy + 1) * w + ix + 1] += a * fx * fy;
}
static float ref_h(const float *z, int w, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y; float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    float a = z[iy * w + ix], b = z[iy * w + ix + 1], c = z[(iy + 1) * w + ix], d = z[(iy + 1) * w + ix + 1];
    *gx = (b - a) * (1 - fy) + (d - c) * fy; *gy = (c - a) * (1 - fx) + (d - b) * fx;
    return a * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + b * fx * (1 - fy) + c * (1 - fx) * fy + d * fx * fy;
}
static float ref_drop(float *z, int w, int h, float x, float y) {
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, sp = 1, wa = 1, sed = 0, gx, gy, t1, t2;
    for (int s = 0; s < 30; s++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y; float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
        float hh = ref_h(z, w, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * 0.05f - gx * 0.95f; dy = dy * 0.05f - gy * 0.95f;
        float l = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy); if (l == 0) break;
        dx /= l; dy /= l; x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= w - 1 || y >= h - 1) break;
        float dh = ref_h(z, w, x, y, &t1, &t2) - hh;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * sp * wa * 4, 0.01f);
        if (sed > cap || dh > 0) { float a = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sed) : (sed - cap) * 0.3f; sed -= a; ref_add(z, w, ix, iy, fx, fy, a); }
        else { float a = fminf((cap - sed) * 0.3f, -dh); ref_add(z, w, ix, iy, fx, fy, -a); sed += a; }
        sp = sqrtf(fmaxf(0, sp * sp - dh * 4)); wa *= 0.99f;
    }
    return sed;
}
int main(void) {
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    CHECK(p.inertia == 0.05f && p.capacity == 4 && p.min_capacity == 0.01f && p.deposition == 0.3f);
    CHECK(p.erosion == 0.3f && p.evaporation == 0.01f && p.gravity == 4 && p.max_steps == 30);

    Heightmap b = hm_new(4, 4);
    hm_add_bilinear(&b, 1, 2, 0.25f, 0.5f, 2.0f);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_get(&b, 1, 2) - 0.75f) < 1e-6f && fabsf(hm_get(&b, 2, 2) - 0.25f) < 1e-6f);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_get(&b, 1, 3) - 0.75f) < 1e-6f && fabsf(hm_get(&b, 2, 3) - 0.25f) < 1e-6f);
    CHECK(hm_get(&b, 0, 0) == 0);
    hm_free(&b);

    /* flat ground: zero gradient, the droplet stops immediately */
    Heightmap f = hm_new(8, 8);
    CHECK(erode_droplet(&f, &p, 3.5f, 3.5f) == 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) CHECK(f.z[i] == 0);
    hm_free(&f);

    /* start outside: nothing happens */
    Heightmap r = hm_new(30, 10);
    for (int y = 0; y < 10; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 30; x++) r.z[y * 30 + x] = 1 - x * 0.02f;
    CHECK(erode_droplet(&r, &p, 29.0f, 5.0f) == 0);
    CHECK(erode_droplet(&r, &p, -0.5f, 5.0f) == 0);
    CHECK(erode_droplet(&r, &p, 3.0f, 9.0f) == 0);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_get(&r, 5, 5) - 0.9f) < 1e-7f);

    /* a ramp sloping down in +x: the droplet runs straight downhill, eroding its row, and falls off the edge */
    float sed = erode_droplet(&r, &p, 2.5f, 4.5f);
    CHECK(sed > 0);
    CHECK(hm_get(&r, 10, 4) < 1 - 10 * 0.02f - 1e-4f);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_get(&r, 10, 1) - (1 - 10 * 0.02f)) < 1e-7f);
    CHECK(fabsf(hm_get(&r, 1, 4) - (1 - 0.02f)) < 1e-7f);
    float rz[300];
    for (int y = 0; y < 10; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 30; x++) rz[y * 30 + x] = 1 - x * 0.02f;
    float rsed = ref_drop(rz, 30, 10, 2.5f, 4.5f);
    CHECK(fabsf(sed - rsed) < 1e-5f);
    float md = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++) md = fmaxf(md, fabsf(rz[i] - r.z[i]));
    CHECK(md < 1e-5f);
    hm_free(&r);

    /* a pit: the droplet runs in and deposits when it starts climbing out */
    Heightmap pit = hm_new(21, 21);
    for (int y = 0; y < 21; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 21; x++) pit.z[y * 21 + x] = ((x - 10) * (x - 10) + (y - 10) * (y - 10)) * 0.001f;
    float pz[441]; memcpy(pz, pit.z, sizeof pz);
    float s1 = erode_droplet(&pit, &p, 3.3f, 4.6f), s2 = ref_drop(pz, 21, 21, 3.3f, 4.6f);
    CHECK(fabsf(s1 - s2) < 1e-5f);
    md = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 441; i++) md = fmaxf(md, fabsf(pz[i] - pit.z[i]));
    CHECK(md < 1e-5f);
    hm_free(&pit);

    /* noise terrain: same as the reference for a handful of droplets */
    Heightmap t = hm_new(64, 64);
    hm_fbm(&t, 5, 0.05f, 5);
    hm_normalize(&t);
    float *tz = malloc(sizeof(float) * 64 * 64);
    memcpy(tz, t.z, sizeof(float) * 64 * 64);
    float sa = 0, sb = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        float x = hash01(i, 7, 3) * 63, y = hash01(i, 8, 3) * 63;
        sa += erode_droplet(&t, &p, x, y); sb += ref_drop(tz, 64, 64, x, y);
    }
    CHECK(fabsf(sa - sb) < 1e-4f);
    double diff = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 64 * 64; i++) diff += fabsf(t.z[i] - tz[i]);
    CHECK(diff < 1e-3);
    free(tz);
    hm_free(&t);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_erode
title: Terrain 7: thousands of droplets with an erosion brush
continues: hm_droplet
--- prompt
Eroding only the 4 corners of a cell digs one-cell-wide pits. Lague erodes with a **brush**: the eroded amount is spread over all cells within a radius, weighted by `radius - distance`. Deposits stay bilinear (they fill pits precisely).

```c
typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;
Brush brush_new(int radius);
void brush_free(Brush *b);
void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius);
```

**`brush_new`**: for `dy` from `-radius` to `radius`, then `dx` from `-radius` to `radius` (that order), include every offset whose distance `d = sqrtf(dx*dx + dy*dy)` is `< radius`, with weight `radius - d`; then divide all weights by their sum so they add up to 1. Allocate the three arrays once, sized for the worst case `(2r+1)²`.

**`hm_erode`**: build the brush **once**, then run `droplets` droplets. Droplet `i` starts at `x = hash01(i, 0, seed) * (w - 1)`, `y = hash01(i, 1, seed) * (h - 1)`. Each droplet follows exactly the step 6 rules, except the **erosion** branch: with `amount = min((cap - sediment) * erosion, -dh)`, for each brush offset `k` with the cell `(ix + dx[k], iy + dy[k])` inside the map:

```text
want  = amount * w[k]
taken = cell height < want ? cell height : want      (never dig below 0)
cell height -= taken;  sediment += taken
```

Brush cells outside the map are skipped (their share is simply not eroded). Free the brush before returning.

The tidy way to avoid two copies of the droplet loop is to move the body of `erode_droplet` into a function that takes a `const Brush *` (or `NULL` for the bilinear erosion of step 6) and have `erode_droplet` call it with `NULL`.

> **Performance:** the brush is the same for every droplet and step, so its `sqrtf`s and its allocation happen once per `hm_erode` call. Building it per droplet would mean 100,000 allocations and millions of square roots for nothing; this is the "precompute what does not change" habit in its simplest form.

--- stubs
typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    (void)radius;
    Brush b = {0, NULL, NULL, NULL};
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    (void)b;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    (void)m; (void)p; (void)seed; (void)droplets; (void)radius;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

int main(void) {
    Heightmap m = hm_new(128, 128);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 40, 6);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    hm_island(&m, 0.25f, 0.95f);
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    hm_erode(&m, &p, 1, 20000, 3);
    printf("P2\n128 128\n255\n");                 /* height plus a quick slope shading */
    for (int y = 0; y < 128; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < 128; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < 127 ? x + 1 : 127, yu = y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0, yd = y < 127 ? y + 1 : 127;
            float shade = (hm_get(&m, xl, y) - hm_get(&m, xr, y) + hm_get(&m, x, yu) - hm_get(&m, x, yd)) * 8;
            printf("%d%c", (int)clampf(255 * (0.15f + 0.6f * hm_get(&m, x, y) + shade), 0, 255), x == 127 ? '\n' : ' ');
        }
    hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
For radius 1 the brush is a single offset `(0, 0)` with weight 1 (the 4 neighbors are at distance exactly 1, not `< 1`). Radius 2 has 9 offsets.

--- hint
If you refactor, keep `erode_droplet` itself working: `float erode_droplet(...) { return droplet_run(m, p, NULL, x, y); }`.

--- harness
typedef struct { int n; int dx[64], dy[64]; float w[64]; } RefBrush;
static void ref_brush(RefBrush *b, int r) {
    float s = 0; b->n = 0;
    for (int y = -r; y <= r; y++) for (int x = -r; x <= r; x++) {
        float d = sqrtf((float)(x * x + y * y));
        if (d < r) { b->dx[b->n] = x; b->dy[b->n] = y; b->w[b->n] = r - d; s += r - d; b->n++; }
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < b->n; i++) b->w[i] /= s;
}
static float rh(const float *z, int w, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y; float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    float a = z[iy * w + ix], b = z[iy * w + ix + 1], c = z[(iy + 1) * w + ix], d = z[(iy + 1) * w + ix + 1];
    *gx = (b - a) * (1 - fy) + (d - c) * fy; *gy = (c - a) * (1 - fx) + (d - b) * fx;
    return a * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + b * fx * (1 - fy) + c * (1 - fx) * fy + d * fx * fy;
}
static void ref_erode(float *z, int w, int h, uint32_t seed, int drops, int r) {
    RefBrush br; ref_brush(&br, r);
    for (int i = 0; i < drops; i++) {
        float x = hash01(i, 0, seed) * (w - 1), y = hash01(i, 1, seed) * (h - 1);
        if (!(x < w - 1 && y < h - 1)) continue;
        float dx = 0, dy = 0, sp = 1, wa = 1, sed = 0, gx, gy, t1, t2;
        for (int s = 0; s < 30; s++) {
            int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y; float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
            float hh = rh(z, w, x, y, &gx, &gy);
            dx = dx * 0.05f - gx * 0.95f; dy = dy * 0.05f - gy * 0.95f;
            float l = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy); if (l == 0) break;
            dx /= l; dy /= l; x += dx; y += dy;
            if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= w - 1 || y >= h - 1) break;
            float dh = rh(z, w, x, y, &t1, &t2) - hh;
            float cap = fmaxf(-dh * sp * wa * 4, 0.01f);
            if (sed > cap || dh > 0) {
                float a = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sed) : (sed - cap) * 0.3f; sed -= a;
                z[iy * w + ix] += a * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy); z[iy * w + ix + 1] += a * fx * (1 - fy);
                z[(iy + 1) * w + ix] += a * (1 - fx) * fy; z[(iy + 1) * w + ix + 1] += a * fx * fy;
            } else {
                float a = fminf((cap - sed) * 0.3f, -dh);
                for (int k = 0; k < br.n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + br.dx[k], cy = iy + br.dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= w || cy >= h) continue;
                    float want = a * br.w[k], *c = &z[cy * w + cx], tk = *c < want ? *c : want;
                    *c -= tk; sed += tk;
                }
            }
            sp = sqrtf(fmaxf(0, sp * sp - dh * 4)); wa *= 0.99f;
        }
    }
}
int main(void) {
    Brush b1 = brush_new(1);
    CHECK_INT(b1.n, 1);
    if (b1.n == 1) CHECK(b1.dx[0] == 0 && b1.dy[0] == 0 && fabsf(b1.w[0] - 1) < 1e-6f);
    brush_free(&b1);
    for (int r = 2; r <= 4; r++) {
        Brush b = brush_new(r);
        RefBrush rb; ref_brush(&rb, r);
        CHECK_INT(b.n, rb.n);
        if (b.n == rb.n) {
            float s = 0; int ok = 1;
            for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) {
                s += b.w[i];
                if (b.dx[i] != rb.dx[i] || b.dy[i] != rb.dy[i] || fabsf(b.w[i] - rb.w[i]) > 1e-6f) ok = 0;
            }
            CHECK(ok);
            CHECK(fabsf(s - 1) < 1e-5f);
        }
        brush_free(&b);
    }
    Brush b3 = brush_new(3);
    CHECK_INT(b3.n, 25);
    if (b3.n == 25) CHECK(b3.dx[0] == -2 && b3.dy[0] == -2 && b3.dx[24] == 2 && b3.dy[24] == 2);
    brush_free(&b3);

    /* step 6's droplet must still work */
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    Heightmap r = hm_new(30, 10);
    for (int y = 0; y < 10; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 30; x++) r.z[y * 30 + x] = 1 - x * 0.02f;
    CHECK(erode_droplet(&r, &p, 2.5f, 4.5f) > 0);
    hm_free(&r);

    /* matches the reference */
    Heightmap t = hm_new(64, 48);
    hm_fbm(&t, 21, 0.06f, 5);
    hm_normalize(&t);
    float *ref = malloc(sizeof(float) * 64 * 48);
    memcpy(ref, t.z, sizeof(float) * 64 * 48);
    double before = 0, after = 0, diff = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 64 * 48; i++) before += t.z[i];
    hm_erode(&t, &p, 77, 3000, 3);
    ref_erode(ref, 64, 48, 77, 3000, 3);
    int neg = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 64 * 48; i++) { after += t.z[i]; diff += fabsf(t.z[i] - ref[i]); neg += t.z[i] < -1e-6f; }
    CHECK(diff / (64 * 48) < 1e-4);
    CHECK(after < before);
    CHECK(before - after < 0.2 * before);
    CHECK_INT(neg, 0);
    float mx = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 64 * 48; i++) mx = fmaxf(mx, fabsf(t.z[i] - ref[i]));
    CHECK(mx < 0.02f);

    /* deterministic, seed-dependent */
    memcpy(ref, t.z, sizeof(float) * 64 * 48);
    Heightmap t2 = hm_new(64, 48);
    hm_fbm(&t2, 21, 0.06f, 5);
    hm_normalize(&t2);
    hm_erode(&t2, &p, 77, 3000, 3);
    CHECK(memcmp(t2.z, ref, sizeof(float) * 64 * 48) == 0);
    hm_fbm(&t2, 21, 0.06f, 5);
    hm_normalize(&t2);
    hm_erode(&t2, &p, 78, 3000, 3);
    CHECK(memcmp(t2.z, ref, sizeof(float) * 64 * 48) != 0);
    free(ref);
    hm_free(&t); hm_free(&t2);

    /* a big run */
    Heightmap big = hm_new(256, 256);
    hm_fbm(&big, 2, 0.01f, 6);
    hm_normalize(&big);
    hm_erode(&big, &p, 5, 60000, 3);
    CHECK(big.z[300] == big.z[300]);
    hm_free(&big);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_normals
title: Terrain 8: normals
continues: hm_erode
--- prompt
Implement `void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out)`. `out` has room for `3 * w * h` floats; write the normal of point `(x, y)` to `out[3 * (y * w + x) + 0..2]`.

```text
dx = (z(x+1, y) - z(x-1, y)) * 0.5 * height_scale
dy = (z(x, y+1) - z(x, y-1)) * 0.5 * height_scale
n  = normalize(-dx, -dy, 1)
```

At the border, clamp the neighbor coordinate into the map (so at `x = 0`, `z(x-1, y)` is `z(0, y)`), and still multiply by 0.5. `height_scale` is "world height units per grid cell": a heightmap in `[0, 1]` meant to be 40 cells tall uses 40.

> **Performance:** the output is caller-allocated, so computing normals for every frame or every tile allocates nothing; and it is interleaved `xyz` because the next steps read all three components of one normal together. Clamping by branching on the border inside the inner loop is fine here; the faster pattern is to handle row 0, the last row and the first/last columns in separate small loops and run the interior with no checks at all.

--- stubs
void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    (void)m; (void)height_scale; (void)out;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    float s = 0.5f * height_scale;
    for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * w;
        const float *up = m->z + (size_t)(y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0) * w;
        const float *dn = m->z + (size_t)(y < h - 1 ? y + 1 : h - 1) * w;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < w - 1 ? x + 1 : w - 1;
            float dx = (row[xr] - row[xl]) * s, dy = (dn[x] - up[x]) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            float *o = out + 3 * ((size_t)y * w + x);
            o[0] = -dx * il; o[1] = -dy * il; o[2] = il;
        }
    }
}

int main(void) {
    /* normals as colors: the classic "normal map" look (x -> red, y -> green, z -> blue) */
    Heightmap m = hm_new(128, 128);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 40, 6);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    hm_island(&m, 0.25f, 0.95f);
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    hm_erode(&m, &p, 1, 20000, 3);
    float *n = malloc(sizeof(float) * 3 * 128 * 128);
    hm_normals(&m, 40, n);
    Image im = img_new(128, 128);
    for (int i = 0; i < 128 * 128; i++)
        img_set(&im, i % 128, i / 128, (int)(127.5f + 127.5f * n[3 * i]), (int)(127.5f + 127.5f * n[3 * i + 1]), (int)(255 * n[3 * i + 2]));
    img_write_ppm(&im);
    img_free(&im); free(n); hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
Precompute the clamped row pointers for `y - 1` and `y + 1` once per row.

--- harness
int main(void) {
    Heightmap p = hm_new(6, 5);
    for (int y = 0; y < 5; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 6; x++) p.z[y * 6 + x] = 0.1f * x + 0.05f * y;
    float n[90];
    hm_normals(&p, 10, n);
    float dx = 0.1f * 10, dy = 0.05f * 10, l = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
    int i = 3 * (2 * 6 + 3);
    CHECK(fabsf(n[i] + dx / l) < 1e-5f && fabsf(n[i + 1] + dy / l) < 1e-5f && fabsf(n[i + 2] - 1 / l) < 1e-5f);
    /* border: one-sided difference still multiplied by 0.5 */
    i = 3 * (0 * 6 + 0);
    float bdx = 0.05f * 10, bdy = 0.025f * 10, bl = sqrtf(bdx * bdx + bdy * bdy + 1);
    CHECK(fabsf(n[i] + bdx / bl) < 1e-5f && fabsf(n[i + 1] + bdy / bl) < 1e-5f && fabsf(n[i + 2] - 1 / bl) < 1e-5f);
    i = 3 * (4 * 6 + 5);
    CHECK(fabsf(n[i] + bdx / bl) < 1e-5f && fabsf(n[i + 1] + bdy / bl) < 1e-5f);
    i = 3 * (4 * 6 + 2);
    CHECK(fabsf(n[i] + dx / sqrtf(dx * dx + bdy * bdy + 1)) < 1e-5f);
    hm_free(&p);

    Heightmap f = hm_new(3, 3);
    float fn[27];
    hm_normals(&f, 50, fn);
    for (int k = 0; k < 9; k++) CHECK(fn[3 * k] == 0 && fn[3 * k + 1] == 0 && fabsf(fn[3 * k + 2] - 1) < 1e-7f);
    hm_free(&f);

    Heightmap t = hm_new(50, 40);
    hm_fbm(&t, 4, 0.1f, 5);
    float *tn = malloc(sizeof(float) * 3 * 50 * 40);
    hm_normals(&t, 30, tn);
    int unit = 1, up = 1;
    for (int k = 0; k < 2000; k++) {
        float L = tn[3 * k] * tn[3 * k] + tn[3 * k + 1] * tn[3 * k + 1] + tn[3 * k + 2] * tn[3 * k + 2];
        if (fabsf(L - 1) > 1e-5f) unit = 0;
        if (tn[3 * k + 2] <= 0) up = 0;
    }
    CHECK(unit && up);
    int x = 17, y = 23;
    float ex = (hm_get(&t, x + 1, y) - hm_get(&t, x - 1, y)) * 15, ey = (hm_get(&t, x, y + 1) - hm_get(&t, x, y - 1)) * 15;
    float el = sqrtf(ex * ex + ey * ey + 1);
    i = 3 * (y * 50 + x);
    CHECK(fabsf(tn[i] + ex / el) < 1e-5f && fabsf(tn[i + 1] + ey / el) < 1e-5f && fabsf(tn[i + 2] - 1 / el) < 1e-5f);
    x = 49; y = 39;
    ex = (hm_get(&t, 49, 39) - hm_get(&t, 48, 39)) * 15; ey = (hm_get(&t, 49, 39) - hm_get(&t, 49, 38)) * 15;
    el = sqrtf(ex * ex + ey * ey + 1);
    i = 3 * (39 * 50 + 49);
    CHECK(fabsf(tn[i] + ex / el) < 1e-5f && fabsf(tn[i + 1] + ey / el) < 1e-5f);
    free(tn);
    hm_free(&t);

    Heightmap one = hm_new(1, 1);
    one.z[0] = 5;
    float on[3];
    hm_normals(&one, 10, on);
    CHECK(on[0] == 0 && on[1] == 0 && fabsf(on[2] - 1) < 1e-7f);
    hm_free(&one);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_shade
title: Terrain 9: coloring and hillshade
continues: hm_normals
--- prompt
Turn heights and normals into a map image.

`Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg)`: with angles converted to radians, `(cos(alt) cos(az), cos(alt) sin(az), sin(alt))`. Azimuth 0 points along +x, 90 along +y; altitude 90 is straight up.

`void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out)` (`out` is `w × h`, `light` is unit length). For each point with height `h`, normal `n` and slope `s = 1 - n.z`, pick a base color:

| condition (first match) | base color (r, g, b) |
|---|---|
| `h < sea` | water: lerp from (20, 50, 120) to (40, 110, 170) by `t = h / sea`; also use `n = (0, 0, 1)` |
| `h < sea + 0.02` | sand (200, 185, 130) |
| `s > 0.3` | rock (115, 105, 95) |
| `h > 0.8` | snow (240, 240, 245) |
| otherwise | grass: lerp from (80, 140, 60) to (50, 95, 40) by `t = (h - sea) / (0.8 - sea)` |

Then `lit = 0.35 + 0.65 * max(0, n · light)`, and each channel is `(int)(base * lit + 0.5f)`, capped at 255.

The thresholds and colors are art direction you would tune; the structure (height bands, slope override for cliffs, lighting multiplied on top) is how most terrain shaders start. Real ones blend between bands with `smoothstep` and noise instead of hard cuts.

--- stubs
Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg) {
    (void)azimuth_deg; (void)altitude_deg;
    return v3(0, 0, 0);
}

void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out) {
    (void)m; (void)normals; (void)light; (void)sea; (void)out;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
