                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    float s = 0.5f * height_scale;
    for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * w;
        const float *up = m->z + (size_t)(y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0) * w;
        const float *dn = m->z + (size_t)(y < h - 1 ? y + 1 : h - 1) * w;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < w - 1 ? x + 1 : w - 1;
            float dx = (row[xr] - row[xl]) * s, dy = (dn[x] - up[x]) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            float *o = out + 3 * ((size_t)y * w + x);
            o[0] = -dx * il; o[1] = -dy * il; o[2] = il;
        }
    }
}

Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg) {
    float az = azimuth_deg * 3.14159265f / 180, alt = altitude_deg * 3.14159265f / 180;
    return v3(cosf(alt) * cosf(az), cosf(alt) * sinf(az), sinf(alt));
}

void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float h = m->z[i], r, g, b, t;
        Vec3 nr = v3(normals[3 * i], normals[3 * i + 1], normals[3 * i + 2]);
        float slope = 1 - nr.z;
        if (h < sea) {
            t = h / sea;
            r = lerpf(20, 40, t); g = lerpf(50, 110, t); b = lerpf(120, 170, t);
            nr = v3(0, 0, 1);
        } else if (h < sea + 0.02f) { r = 200; g = 185; b = 130; }
        else if (slope > 0.3f) { r = 115; g = 105; b = 95; }
        else if (h > 0.8f) { r = 240; g = 240; b = 245; }
        else {
            t = (h - sea) / (0.8f - sea);
            r = lerpf(80, 50, t); g = lerpf(140, 95, t); b = lerpf(60, 40, t);
        }
        float lit = 0.35f + 0.65f * fmaxf(0, v3_dot(nr, light));
        unsigned char *px = out->px + 3 * i;
        px[0] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(r * lit + 0.5f));
        px[1] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(g * lit + 0.5f));
        px[2] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(b * lit + 0.5f));
    }
}

int main(void) {
    Heightmap m = hm_new(128, 128);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 40, 6);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    hm_island(&m, 0.25f, 0.95f);
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    hm_erode(&m, &p, 1, 30000, 3);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    float *n = malloc(sizeof(float) * 3 * 128 * 128);
    hm_normals(&m, 40, n);
    Image im = img_new(128, 128);
    hm_shade(&m, n, light_dir(225, 45), 0.3f, &im);    /* light from the north-west */
    img_write_ppm(&im);
    img_free(&im); free(n); hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
Normalize nothing in `hm_shade`: the normals and the light are already unit length.

--- hint
For water, compute the color first, then replace the normal before lighting.

--- harness
static void ref_px(float h, float nx, float ny, float nz, Vec3 L, float sea, int out[3]) {
    float c[3], t;
    if (h < sea) { t = h / sea; c[0] = 20 + 20 * t; c[1] = 50 + 60 * t; c[2] = 120 + 50 * t; nx = 0; ny = 0; nz = 1; }
    else if (h < sea + 0.02f) { c[0] = 200; c[1] = 185; c[2] = 130; }
    else if (1 - nz > 0.3f) { c[0] = 115; c[1] = 105; c[2] = 95; }
    else if (h > 0.8f) { c[0] = 240; c[1] = 240; c[2] = 245; }
    else { t = (h - sea) / (0.8f - sea); c[0] = 80 - 30 * t; c[1] = 140 - 45 * t; c[2] = 60 - 20 * t; }
    float lit = 0.35f + 0.65f * fmaxf(0, nx * L.x + ny * L.y + nz * L.z);
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) { int v = (int)(c[k] * lit + 0.5f); out[k] = v > 255 ? 255 : v; }
}
static int px_near(const Image *im, int x, int y, const int want[3]) {
    const unsigned char *p = im->px + 3 * ((size_t)y * im->w + x);
    for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++) if (abs(p[k] - want[k]) > 1) return 0;
    return 1;
}
int main(void) {
    Vec3 L = light_dir(0, 90);
    CHECK(fabsf(L.x) < 1e-6f && fabsf(L.y) < 1e-6f && fabsf(L.z - 1) < 1e-6f);
    L = light_dir(90, 0);
    CHECK(fabsf(L.x) < 1e-6f && fabsf(L.y - 1) < 1e-6f && fabsf(L.z) < 1e-6f);
    L = light_dir(225, 45);
    CHECK(fabsf(L.x + 0.5f) < 1e-5f && fabsf(L.y + 0.5f) < 1e-5f && fabsf(L.z - 0.70710678f) < 1e-5f);

    /* hand-made 5x1 map: one point per band */
    Heightmap m = hm_new(6, 1);
    float hs[6] = {0.15f, 0.31f, 0.5f, 0.5f, 0.9f, 0.9f};
    memcpy(m.z, hs, sizeof hs);
    float n[18] = {0.6f, 0, 0.8f,  0, 0, 1,  0, 0, 1,  0.8f, 0, 0.6f,  0, 0.6f, 0.8f,  0, 0, 1};
    Image im = img_new(6, 1);
    Vec3 l2 = v3_norm(v3(1, 0, 1));
    hm_shade(&m, n, l2, 0.3f, &im);
    for (int x = 0; x < 6; x++) {
        int want[3];
        ref_px(hs[x], n[3 * x], n[3 * x + 1], n[3 * x + 2], l2, 0.3f, want);
        CHECK(px_near(&im, x, 0, want));
    }
    const unsigned char *p = im.px;
    CHECK(abs(p[0] - (int)(30 * (0.35f + 0.65f * 0.70710678f) + 0.5f)) <= 1);
    CHECK(abs(p[3 * 1 + 0] - (int)(200 * (0.35f + 0.65f * 0.70710678f) + 0.5f)) <= 1);
    CHECK(abs(p[3 * 3 + 0] - (int)(115 * (0.35f + 0.65f * (0.8f + 0.6f) * 0.70710678f) + 0.5f)) <= 1);
    CHECK(abs(p[3 * 5 + 2] - (int)(245 * (0.35f + 0.65f * 0.70710678f) + 0.5f)) <= 1);
    img_free(&im); hm_free(&m);

    /* a light from below: everything at ambient */
    Heightmap g = hm_new(2, 1);
    g.z[0] = 0.5f; g.z[1] = 0.5f;
    float gn[6] = {0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1};
    Image gi = img_new(2, 1);
    hm_shade(&g, gn, v3(0, 0, -1), 0.2f, &gi);
    float t = (0.5f - 0.2f) / 0.6f;
    CHECK(abs(gi.px[1] - (int)((140 - 45 * t) * 0.35f + 0.5f)) <= 1);
    img_free(&gi); hm_free(&g);

    /* real terrain against the reference */
    Heightmap t2 = hm_new(48, 40);
    hm_fbm(&t2, 8, 0.07f, 5);
    hm_normalize(&t2);
    float *tn = malloc(sizeof(float) * 3 * 48 * 40);
    hm_normals(&t2, 25, tn);
    Image ti = img_new(48, 40);
    Vec3 l3 = light_dir(135, 35);
    hm_shade(&t2, tn, l3, 0.35f, &ti);
    int bad = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 48 * 40; i++) {
        int want[3];
        ref_px(t2.z[i], tn[3 * i], tn[3 * i + 1], tn[3 * i + 2], l3, 0.35f, want);
        if (!px_near(&ti, i % 48, i / 48, want)) bad++;
    }
    CHECK_INT(bad, 0);
    free(tn); img_free(&ti); hm_free(&t2);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_mesh
title: Terrain 10: a triangle mesh
continues: hm_shade
--- prompt
Turn the heightmap into an indexed triangle mesh for a GPU or an OBJ file.

```c
typedef struct { int nverts, ntris; float *pos, *nrm; uint32_t *idx; } Mesh;
int hm_mesh(const Heightmap *m, int step, float cell, float hscale, Mesh *out);
void mesh_free(Mesh *me);
```

`step` subsamples the map (level of detail): use every `step`-th point. It must divide both `w - 1` and `h - 1`, and `w, h >= 2`; otherwise return 0 without allocating. With `gw = (w - 1) / step + 1` and `gh = (h - 1) / step + 1`:

- vertex `(i, j)` (`0 <= i < gw`, `0 <= j < gh`) has index `j * gw + i`, from map point `(x, y) = (i * step, j * step)`, at position `(x * cell, y * cell, z(x, y) * hscale)` in `pos[3 * index ...]`;
- its normal `nrm[3 * index ...]` is the step 8 normal at `(x, y)` with `height_scale = hscale / cell` (central differences with neighbors 1 map cell away, clamped);
- quad `(i, j)` (`i < gw - 1`, `j < gh - 1`) with corners `a = j*gw + i`, `b = a + 1`, `c = a + gw`, `d = c + 1` becomes triangles `(a, b, d)` and `(a, d, c)`, quads in row-major order (`j` outer). That winding is counter-clockwise seen from above.

Allocate `pos`, `nrm` and `idx` **once each**, with sizes computed from `gw` and `gh` before the loops. Return 1. `mesh_free` frees and zeroes the struct.

The large test builds a 1025 × 1025 mesh (1 million vertices, 6 million indices). Growing arrays one element at a time with `realloc` copies the whole array on most calls; that test will time out.

--- stubs
typedef struct { int nverts, ntris; float *pos, *nrm; uint32_t *idx; } Mesh;

int hm_mesh(const Heightmap *m, int step, float cell, float hscale, Mesh *out) {
    (void)m; (void)step; (void)cell; (void)hscale;
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    return 0;
}

void mesh_free(Mesh *me) {
    (void)me;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    float s = 0.5f * height_scale;
    for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * w;
        const float *up = m->z + (size_t)(y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0) * w;
        const float *dn = m->z + (size_t)(y < h - 1 ? y + 1 : h - 1) * w;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < w - 1 ? x + 1 : w - 1;
            float dx = (row[xr] - row[xl]) * s, dy = (dn[x] - up[x]) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            float *o = out + 3 * ((size_t)y * w + x);
            o[0] = -dx * il; o[1] = -dy * il; o[2] = il;
        }
    }
}

Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg) {
    float az = azimuth_deg * 3.14159265f / 180, alt = altitude_deg * 3.14159265f / 180;
    return v3(cosf(alt) * cosf(az), cosf(alt) * sinf(az), sinf(alt));
}

void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float h = m->z[i], r, g, b, t;
        Vec3 nr = v3(normals[3 * i], normals[3 * i + 1], normals[3 * i + 2]);
        float slope = 1 - nr.z;
        if (h < sea) {
            t = h / sea;
            r = lerpf(20, 40, t); g = lerpf(50, 110, t); b = lerpf(120, 170, t);
            nr = v3(0, 0, 1);
        } else if (h < sea + 0.02f) { r = 200; g = 185; b = 130; }
        else if (slope > 0.3f) { r = 115; g = 105; b = 95; }
        else if (h > 0.8f) { r = 240; g = 240; b = 245; }
        else {
            t = (h - sea) / (0.8f - sea);
            r = lerpf(80, 50, t); g = lerpf(140, 95, t); b = lerpf(60, 40, t);
        }
        float lit = 0.35f + 0.65f * fmaxf(0, v3_dot(nr, light));
        unsigned char *px = out->px + 3 * i;
        px[0] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(r * lit + 0.5f));
        px[1] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(g * lit + 0.5f));
        px[2] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(b * lit + 0.5f));
    }
}

typedef struct { int nverts, ntris; float *pos, *nrm; uint32_t *idx; } Mesh;

int hm_mesh(const Heightmap *m, int step, float cell, float hscale, Mesh *out) {
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    if (step < 1 || m->w < 2 || m->h < 2 || (m->w - 1) % step || (m->h - 1) % step) return 0;
    int gw = (m->w - 1) / step + 1, gh = (m->h - 1) / step + 1;
    size_t nv = (size_t)gw * gh, ni = (size_t)6 * (gw - 1) * (gh - 1);
    out->pos = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->nrm = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->idx = malloc(ni * sizeof(uint32_t));
    out->nverts = (int)nv;
    out->ntris = (int)(ni / 3);
    float s = 0.5f * hscale / cell;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw; i++) {
            int x = i * step, y = j * step;
            size_t v = (size_t)j * gw + i;
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < m->w - 1 ? x + 1 : m->w - 1;
            int yu = y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0, yd = y < m->h - 1 ? y + 1 : m->h - 1;
            float dx = (hm_get(m, xr, y) - hm_get(m, xl, y)) * s;
            float dy = (hm_get(m, x, yd) - hm_get(m, x, yu)) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            out->pos[3 * v] = x * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 1] = y * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 2] = hm_get(m, x, y) * hscale;
            out->nrm[3 * v] = -dx * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 1] = -dy * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 2] = il;
        }
    uint32_t *ix = out->idx;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh - 1; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw - 1; i++) {
            uint32_t a = (uint32_t)(j * gw + i), b = a + 1, c = a + (uint32_t)gw, d = c + 1;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = b; *ix++ = d;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = d; *ix++ = c;
        }
    return 1;
}

void mesh_free(Mesh *me) {
    free(me->pos); free(me->nrm); free(me->idx);
    memset(me, 0, sizeof *me);
}

int main(void) {
    /* a wireframe of the mesh's triangles, projected top-down: LOD step 8 */
    Heightmap m = hm_new(129, 129);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 40, 6);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    Mesh me;
    hm_mesh(&m, 8, 1.0f, 40.0f, &me);
    Image im = img_new(128, 128);
    for (int t = 0; t < me.ntris * 3; t++) {
        const float *a = me.pos + 3 * me.idx[t], *b = me.pos + 3 * me.idx[t % 3 == 2 ? t - 2 : t + 1];
        for (int k = 0; k <= 64; k++) {
            float u = k / 64.0f, x = lerpf(a[0], b[0], u), y = lerpf(a[1], b[1], u), z = lerpf(a[2], b[2], u);
            int px = (int)(x * 127 / 128), py = (int)(y * 127 / 128), c = (int)(80 + z * 4);
            img_set(&im, px, py, c / 2, c, 255 - c);
        }
    }
    img_write_ppm(&im);
    img_free(&im); mesh_free(&me); hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
`(size_t)6 * (gw - 1) * (gh - 1)`: do the multiplication in `size_t`.

--- hint
A write cursor (`uint32_t *ix = out->idx; *ix++ = a; ...`) avoids computing `6 * quad + k` by hand.

--- harness
int main(void) {
    Heightmap m = hm_new(3, 2);
    float z[6] = {0, 0.5f, 1, 0.25f, 0.75f, 0.5f};
    memcpy(m.z, z, sizeof z);
    Mesh me;
    CHECK_INT(hm_mesh(&m, 1, 2.0f, 10.0f, &me), 1);
    CHECK_INT(me.nverts, 6);
    CHECK_INT(me.ntris, 4);
    if (me.nverts == 6 && me.ntris == 4) {
        uint32_t want[12] = {0, 1, 4, 0, 4, 3, 1, 2, 5, 1, 5, 4};
        int ok = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) if (me.idx[i] != want[i]) ok = 0;
        CHECK(ok);
        CHECK(me.pos[3 * 4] == 2.0f && me.pos[3 * 4 + 1] == 2.0f && fabsf(me.pos[3 * 4 + 2] - 7.5f) < 1e-6f);
        CHECK(me.pos[3 * 2] == 4.0f && me.pos[3 * 2 + 1] == 0.0f && fabsf(me.pos[3 * 2 + 2] - 10) < 1e-6f);
        float s = 0.5f * 10 / 2;
        float dx = (0.5f - 0.25f) * s, dy = (0.75f - 0.5f) * s, l = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
        CHECK(fabsf(me.nrm[3 * 4] + dx / l) < 1e-5f && fabsf(me.nrm[3 * 4 + 1] + dy / l) < 1e-5f && fabsf(me.nrm[3 * 4 + 2] - 1 / l) < 1e-5f);
        /* counter-clockwise from above: positive z of the cross product */
        int ccw = 1;
        for (int t = 0; t < 4; t++) {
            const float *a = me.pos + 3 * me.idx[3 * t], *b = me.pos + 3 * me.idx[3 * t + 1], *c = me.pos + 3 * me.idx[3 * t + 2];
            if ((b[0] - a[0]) * (c[1] - a[1]) - (b[1] - a[1]) * (c[0] - a[0]) <= 0) ccw = 0;
        }
        CHECK(ccw);
    }
    mesh_free(&me);
    CHECK(me.pos == NULL && me.idx == NULL && me.nverts == 0);
    hm_free(&m);

    Heightmap g = hm_new(9, 5);
    hm_fbm(&g, 3, 0.3f, 3);
    CHECK_INT(hm_mesh(&g, 3, 1, 1, &me), 0);
    CHECK_INT(hm_mesh(&g, 0, 1, 1, &me), 0);
    CHECK_INT(hm_mesh(&g, 4, 1.5f, 8, &me), 1);
    CHECK_INT(me.nverts, 3 * 2);
    CHECK_INT(me.ntris, 4);
    if (me.nverts == 6) {
        CHECK(me.pos[3 * 5] == 12.0f && me.pos[3 * 5 + 1] == 6.0f && fabsf(me.pos[3 * 5 + 2] - hm_get(&g, 8, 4) * 8) < 1e-5f);
        CHECK(me.pos[3 * 1] == 6.0f && me.pos[3 * 1 + 1] == 0.0f);
        float s = 0.5f * 8 / 1.5f;
        float dx = (hm_get(&g, 5, 0) - hm_get(&g, 3, 0)) * s, dy = (hm_get(&g, 4, 1) - hm_get(&g, 4, 0)) * s;
        float l = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
        CHECK(fabsf(me.nrm[3 * 1] + dx / l) < 1e-5f && fabsf(me.nrm[3 * 1 + 1] + dy / l) < 1e-5f);
        CHECK(me.idx[6] == 1 && me.idx[7] == 2 && me.idx[8] == 5 && me.idx[11] == 4);
    }
    mesh_free(&me);
    hm_free(&g);

    Heightmap big = hm_new(1025, 1025);
    hm_fbm(&big, 1, 0.01f, 3);
    CHECK_INT(hm_mesh(&big, 1, 1, 50, &me), 1);
    CHECK_INT(me.nverts, 1025 * 1025);
    CHECK_INT(me.ntris, 2 * 1024 * 1024);
    if (me.ntris == 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
        CHECK(me.idx[6 * 1024 * 1024 - 1] == 1024u * 1025 + 1023);
        CHECK(me.idx[6 * 1024 * 1024 - 2] == 1025u * 1025 - 1);
        unsigned long long sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 6 * 1024 * 1024; i++) sum += me.idx[i];
        CHECK(sum == 3304977334272ULL);
        CHECK(me.pos[3 * (1025 * 1025 - 1)] == 1024.0f);
    }
    mesh_free(&me);
    hm_free(&big);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_obj
title: Terrain 11: OBJ export without quadratic strings
continues: hm_mesh
--- prompt
Write the mesh as Wavefront OBJ text into a caller-supplied buffer:

```c
size_t mesh_obj(const Mesh *me, char *buf, size_t cap);
```

The text is, in this order: one line `v %.3f %.3f %.3f` per vertex, one line `vn %.3f %.3f %.3f` per vertex, then one line `f %u//%u %u//%u %u//%u` per triangle with **1-based** indices (vertex and normal index are the same), each line ending in `\n`.

It follows `snprintf`'s contract: it returns the length of the **full** text (excluding the terminating `'\0'`) even if `cap` is too small, writes at most `cap` bytes including a terminating `'\0'` when `cap > 0`, and `mesh_obj(me, NULL, 0)` is allowed and just measures. So the caller does:

```c
size_t n = mesh_obj(&me, NULL, 0);
char *text = malloc(n + 1);
mesh_obj(&me, text, n + 1);
```

The point of this step: **keep a write position**. `snprintf(buf + len, cap - len, ...)` appends in O(line length). `strcat(buf, line)` (or `sprintf(buf, "%s...", buf, ...)`, which is also undefined behavior) first walks the whole buffer to find its end, so writing `n` lines costs O(n²). The large test writes a 257 × 257 terrain (about 200,000 lines, 9 MB); the quadratic version times out.

--- stubs
size_t mesh_obj(const Mesh *me, char *buf, size_t cap) {
    (void)me;
    if (buf && cap) buf[0] = '\0';
    return 0;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    float s = 0.5f * height_scale;
    for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * w;
        const float *up = m->z + (size_t)(y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0) * w;
        const float *dn = m->z + (size_t)(y < h - 1 ? y + 1 : h - 1) * w;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < w - 1 ? x + 1 : w - 1;
            float dx = (row[xr] - row[xl]) * s, dy = (dn[x] - up[x]) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            float *o = out + 3 * ((size_t)y * w + x);
            o[0] = -dx * il; o[1] = -dy * il; o[2] = il;
        }
    }
}

Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg) {
    float az = azimuth_deg * 3.14159265f / 180, alt = altitude_deg * 3.14159265f / 180;
    return v3(cosf(alt) * cosf(az), cosf(alt) * sinf(az), sinf(alt));
}

void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float h = m->z[i], r, g, b, t;
        Vec3 nr = v3(normals[3 * i], normals[3 * i + 1], normals[3 * i + 2]);
        float slope = 1 - nr.z;
        if (h < sea) {
            t = h / sea;
            r = lerpf(20, 40, t); g = lerpf(50, 110, t); b = lerpf(120, 170, t);
            nr = v3(0, 0, 1);
        } else if (h < sea + 0.02f) { r = 200; g = 185; b = 130; }
        else if (slope > 0.3f) { r = 115; g = 105; b = 95; }
        else if (h > 0.8f) { r = 240; g = 240; b = 245; }
        else {
            t = (h - sea) / (0.8f - sea);
            r = lerpf(80, 50, t); g = lerpf(140, 95, t); b = lerpf(60, 40, t);
        }
        float lit = 0.35f + 0.65f * fmaxf(0, v3_dot(nr, light));
        unsigned char *px = out->px + 3 * i;
        px[0] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(r * lit + 0.5f));
        px[1] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(g * lit + 0.5f));
        px[2] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(b * lit + 0.5f));
    }
}

typedef struct { int nverts, ntris; float *pos, *nrm; uint32_t *idx; } Mesh;

int hm_mesh(const Heightmap *m, int step, float cell, float hscale, Mesh *out) {
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    if (step < 1 || m->w < 2 || m->h < 2 || (m->w - 1) % step || (m->h - 1) % step) return 0;
    int gw = (m->w - 1) / step + 1, gh = (m->h - 1) / step + 1;
    size_t nv = (size_t)gw * gh, ni = (size_t)6 * (gw - 1) * (gh - 1);
    out->pos = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->nrm = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->idx = malloc(ni * sizeof(uint32_t));
    out->nverts = (int)nv;
    out->ntris = (int)(ni / 3);
    float s = 0.5f * hscale / cell;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw; i++) {
            int x = i * step, y = j * step;
            size_t v = (size_t)j * gw + i;
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < m->w - 1 ? x + 1 : m->w - 1;
            int yu = y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0, yd = y < m->h - 1 ? y + 1 : m->h - 1;
            float dx = (hm_get(m, xr, y) - hm_get(m, xl, y)) * s;
            float dy = (hm_get(m, x, yd) - hm_get(m, x, yu)) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            out->pos[3 * v] = x * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 1] = y * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 2] = hm_get(m, x, y) * hscale;
            out->nrm[3 * v] = -dx * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 1] = -dy * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 2] = il;
        }
    uint32_t *ix = out->idx;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh - 1; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw - 1; i++) {
            uint32_t a = (uint32_t)(j * gw + i), b = a + 1, c = a + (uint32_t)gw, d = c + 1;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = b; *ix++ = d;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = d; *ix++ = c;
        }
    return 1;
}

void mesh_free(Mesh *me) {
    free(me->pos); free(me->nrm); free(me->idx);
    memset(me, 0, sizeof *me);
}

size_t mesh_obj(const Mesh *me, char *buf, size_t cap) {
    size_t len = 0;
    if (buf && cap) buf[0] = '\0';
#define OBJ_PUT(...) do { \
        int k_ = snprintf(len < cap ? buf + len : NULL, len < cap ? cap - len : 0, __VA_ARGS__); \
        if (k_ > 0) len += (size_t)k_; \
    } while (0)
    for (int i = 0; i < me->nverts; i++)
        OBJ_PUT("v %.3f %.3f %.3f\n", me->pos[3 * i], me->pos[3 * i + 1], me->pos[3 * i + 2]);
    for (int i = 0; i < me->nverts; i++)
        OBJ_PUT("vn %.3f %.3f %.3f\n", me->nrm[3 * i], me->nrm[3 * i + 1], me->nrm[3 * i + 2]);
    for (int t = 0; t < me->ntris; t++) {
        unsigned a = me->idx[3 * t] + 1, b = me->idx[3 * t + 1] + 1, c = me->idx[3 * t + 2] + 1;
        OBJ_PUT("f %u//%u %u//%u %u//%u\n", a, a, b, b, c, c);
    }
#undef OBJ_PUT
    return len;
}

int main(void) {
    /* prints the OBJ of a tiny 3x3 terrain; paste the text of a bigger one into a .obj file to open it in Blender */
    Heightmap m = hm_new(3, 3);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 0.4f, 3);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    Mesh me;
    hm_mesh(&m, 1, 1.0f, 2.0f, &me);
    size_t n = mesh_obj(&me, NULL, 0);
    char *text = malloc(n + 1);
    mesh_obj(&me, text, n + 1);
    fputs(text, stdout);
    printf("# %zu bytes\n", n);
    free(text); mesh_free(&me); hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
`snprintf(NULL, 0, ...)` is legal and returns the length the text would have; use it when the buffer is already full, so the length keeps counting.

--- hint
A small macro or helper that does "snprintf at `buf + len` with `cap - len` room, then `len += result`" keeps the three loops short.

--- harness
int main(void) {
    Heightmap m = hm_new(2, 2);
    float z[4] = {0, 0.5f, 0.25f, 1};
    memcpy(m.z, z, sizeof z);
    Mesh me;
    hm_mesh(&m, 1, 1, 2, &me);
    char buf[1024];
    size_t n = mesh_obj(&me, buf, sizeof buf);
    const char *want =
        "v 0.000 0.000 0.000\n"
        "v 1.000 0.000 1.000\n"
        "v 0.000 1.000 0.500\n"
        "v 1.000 1.000 2.000\n"
        "vn -0.436 -0.218 0.873\n"
        "vn -0.408 -0.408 0.816\n"
        "vn -0.588 -0.196 0.784\n"
        "vn -0.557 -0.371 0.743\n"
        "f 1//1 2//2 4//4\n"
        "f 1//1 4//4 3//3\n";
    CHECK_STR(buf, want);
    CHECK_INT(n, strlen(want));
    CHECK_INT(mesh_obj(&me, NULL, 0), strlen(want));
    char small[30];
    memset(small, 'X', sizeof small);
    CHECK_INT(mesh_obj(&me, small, 25), strlen(want));
    CHECK_INT(strlen(small), 24);
    CHECK(strncmp(small, want, 24) == 0);
    CHECK(small[25] == 'X');
    char one[1] = {'X'};
    CHECK_INT(mesh_obj(&me, one, 1), strlen(want));
    CHECK(one[0] == '\0');
    mesh_free(&me);
    hm_free(&m);

    Heightmap big = hm_new(257, 257);
    hm_fbm(&big, 5, 0.02f, 4);
    hm_normalize(&big);
    hm_mesh(&big, 1, 0.5f, 20, &me);
    size_t len = mesh_obj(&me, NULL, 0);
    char *text = malloc(len + 1);
    CHECK_INT(mesh_obj(&me, text, len + 1), len);
    CHECK_INT(strlen(text), len);
    int lines = 0, faces = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        if (text[i] == '\n') {
            lines++;
            if (i + 1 < len && text[i + 1] == 'f') faces++;
        }
    }
    CHECK_INT(lines, 2 * 257 * 257 + 2 * 256 * 256);
    CHECK_INT(faces, 2 * 256 * 256);
    const char *last = "f 65791//65791 66049//66049 66048//66048\n";
    CHECK(strcmp(text + len - strlen(last), last) == 0);
    CHECK(strncmp(text, "v 0.000 0.000 ", 14) == 0);
    free(text);
    mesh_free(&me);
    hm_free(&big);
    return TEST_END();
}

=== exercise hm_render
title: Terrain 12: a Voxel Space flyover
continues: hm_obj
--- prompt
The finale: render the eroded, shaded island from the air with the Comanche-style **Voxel Space** algorithm.

```c
typedef struct { float x, y, height, angle, horizon, scale, distance; } VoxelCam;
void hm_render(const Heightmap *m, float hscale, const Image *colors, const VoxelCam *cam, Image *out);
```

`colors` is a `w × h` image of the map (the step 9 output); `out` is any size `W × H`. The camera stands at map position `(x, y)`, `height` world units up, looking along `F = (cos angle, sin angle)` with a 90° field of view. `horizon` is the screen row of the horizon, `scale` the projection's vertical scale (in pixels per unit of height at distance 1), `distance` the far limit. At distance `z` the camera sees a line segment perpendicular to `F`, from `z * (F + G)` to `z * (F - G)` relative to the camera, where `G = (-sin angle, cos angle)` is `F` turned 90°; the columns sweep along it. Exactly:

1. Fill `out` with sky color (150, 190, 230). Allocate a `W`-entry `int` y-buffer once, all set to `H`.
2. `z = 1`, `dz = 1`. While `z < distance`:
   - `pl = (x, y) + z * (cos a - sin a, sin a + cos a)` (column 0), `pr = (x, y) + z * (cos a + sin a, sin a - cos a)`, per-column step `d = (pr - pl) / W`;
   - for each column `sx` from 0 to `W - 1`, with `p = pl + d * sx` (accumulate `p += d` per column): `ix = (int)floorf(p.x)`, `iy = (int)floorf(p.y)`; if inside the map, `row = (int)((height - z_map(ix, iy) * hscale) / z * scale + horizon)`, clamped to `>= 0`; if `row < ybuf[sx]`, paint rows `row .. ybuf[sx] - 1` of column `sx` with `colors(ix, iy)` and set `ybuf[sx] = row`;
   - `z += dz`, `dz += 0.02f`.
3. Free the y-buffer.

Front to back plus the y-buffer means each pixel is painted once, by the nearest terrain that covers it. Far rows get coarser samples because `dz` grows.

(The tests compare against a reference implementation and accept a small fraction of pixels that differ because of float rounding at sample boundaries.)

Click **Run** for the whole pipeline: fBm island, 40,000 droplets, normals, hillshade colors, rendered from the coast.

--- stubs
typedef struct { float x, y, height, angle, horizon, scale, distance; } VoxelCam;

void hm_render(const Heightmap *m, float hscale, const Image *colors, const VoxelCam *cam, Image *out) {
    (void)m; (void)hscale; (void)colors; (void)cam; (void)out;
}

--- solution
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <math.h>

/* ===== From earlier chapters: vectors, hashing, gradient noise, fBm, images ===== */

typedef struct { float x, y, z; } Vec3;
Vec3 v3(float x, float y, float z) { Vec3 r = {x, y, z}; return r; }
Vec3 v3_add(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x + b.x, a.y + b.y, a.z + b.z); }
Vec3 v3_sub(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return v3(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y, a.z - b.z); }
Vec3 v3_scale(Vec3 a, float s) { return v3(a.x * s, a.y * s, a.z * s); }
float v3_dot(Vec3 a, Vec3 b) { return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z; }
Vec3 v3_norm(Vec3 a) { float l = sqrtf(v3_dot(a, a)); return l > 0 ? v3_scale(a, 1.0f / l) : a; }

uint32_t hash32(uint32_t x) {            /* lowbias32 (Chris Wellons) */
    x ^= x >> 16; x *= 0x7feb352dU; x ^= x >> 15; x *= 0x846ca68bU; x ^= x >> 16;
    return x;
}
uint32_t hash2(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {
    return hash32((uint32_t)x * 0x27d4eb2dU ^ hash32((uint32_t)y ^ seed));
}
float hash01(int x, int y, uint32_t seed) {     /* uniform in [0, 1) */
    return (float)(hash2(x, y, seed) >> 8) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
}
float lerpf(float a, float b, float t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
float fade(float t) { return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10); }   /* Perlin 2002 quintic */
float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi) { return v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v; }
float smoothstepf(float e0, float e1, float v) {
    float t = clampf((v - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

static const float GRAD_X[8] = {1, -1, 0, 0, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
static const float GRAD_Y[8] = {0, 0, 1, -1, 0.70710678f, 0.70710678f, -0.70710678f, -0.70710678f};
float grad_dot(uint32_t h, float dx, float dy) { return GRAD_X[h & 7] * dx + GRAD_Y[h & 7] * dy; }

/* 2D gradient (Perlin-style) noise, roughly in [-1, 1], 0 at integer lattice points */
float gnoise2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed) {
    float fx0 = floorf(x), fy0 = floorf(y);
    int ix = (int)fx0, iy = (int)fy0;
    float fx = x - fx0, fy = y - fy0;
    float n00 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy, seed), fx, fy);
    float n10 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy, seed), fx - 1, fy);
    float n01 = grad_dot(hash2(ix, iy + 1, seed), fx, fy - 1);
    float n11 = grad_dot(hash2(ix + 1, iy + 1, seed), fx - 1, fy - 1);
    float u = fade(fx), v = fade(fy);
    return 1.41421356f * lerpf(lerpf(n00, n10, u), lerpf(n01, n11, u), v);
}

/* fractal Brownian motion: octaves of noise, frequency x lacunarity, amplitude x gain; result ~[-1, 1] */
float fbm2(float x, float y, uint32_t seed, int octaves, float lacunarity, float gain) {
    float sum = 0, amp = 1, norm = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        sum += amp * gnoise2(x, y, seed + (uint32_t)i * 7919u);
        norm += amp;
        x *= lacunarity; y *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
    }
    return norm > 0 ? sum / norm : 0;
}

typedef struct { int w, h; unsigned char *px; } Image;      /* RGB, 3 bytes per pixel, row-major */
Image img_new(int w, int h) { Image im = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h * 3, 1)}; return im; }
void img_free(Image *im) { free(im->px); im->px = NULL; }
void img_set(Image *im, int x, int y, int r, int g, int b) {
    unsigned char *p = im->px + ((size_t)y * im->w + x) * 3;
    p[0] = (unsigned char)r; p[1] = (unsigned char)g; p[2] = (unsigned char)b;
}
void img_write_ppm(const Image *im) {                       /* ASCII P3 to stdout: the site draws it */
    printf("P3\n%d %d\n255\n", im->w, im->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < im->h; y++) {
        const unsigned char *p = im->px + (size_t)y * im->w * 3;
        for (int x = 0; x < im->w * 3; x++) printf("%d%c", p[x], x + 1 < im->w * 3 ? ' ' : '\n');
    }
}

/* ===== This chapter: the heightmap, a flat row-major float array ===== */

typedef struct { int w, h; float *z; } Heightmap;           /* height of (x, y) is z[y * w + x] */
Heightmap hm_new(int w, int h) { Heightmap m = {w, h, calloc((size_t)w * h, sizeof(float))}; return m; }
void hm_free(Heightmap *m) { free(m->z); m->z = NULL; }
float hm_get(const Heightmap *m, int x, int y) { return m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x]; }
void hm_write_pgm(const Heightmap *m) {                     /* heights in [0, 1] as a P2 image */
    printf("P2\n%d %d\n255\n", m->w, m->h);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float v = clampf(hm_get(m, x, y), 0, 1);
            printf("%d%c", (int)(v * 255 + 0.5f), x + 1 < m->w ? ' ' : '\n');
        }
}

void hm_fbm(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float scale, int octaves) {
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++)
            m->z[(size_t)y * m->w + x] = fbm2(x * scale, y * scale, seed, octaves, 2.0f, 0.5f);
}

void hm_minmax(const Heightmap *m, float *lo, float *hi) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float a = m->z[0], b = m->z[0];
    for (size_t i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        if (m->z[i] < a) a = m->z[i];
        if (m->z[i] > b) b = m->z[i];
    }
    *lo = a; *hi = b;
}

void hm_normalize(Heightmap *m) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    float lo, hi;
    hm_minmax(m, &lo, &hi);
    if (!(hi > lo)) {
        memset(m->z, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        return;
    }
    float s = 1.0f / (hi - lo);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] = (m->z[i] - lo) * s;
}

int hm_diamond_square(Heightmap *m, uint32_t seed, float roughness) {
    int n = m->w - 1, w = m->w;
    if (m->w != m->h || n < 1 || (n & (n - 1)) != 0) return 0;
    float *z = m->z;
    z[0] = hash01(0, 0, seed);
    z[n] = hash01(n, 0, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w] = hash01(0, n, seed);
    z[(size_t)n * w + n] = hash01(n, n, seed);
    float amp = 0.5f;
    for (int step = n; step > 1; step /= 2, amp *= roughness) {
        int half = step / 2;
        for (int y = half; y < w; y += step)          /* diamond: square centers */
            for (int x = half; x < w; x += step) {
                float avg = (z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x + half] +
                             z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x - half] + z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x + half]) * 0.25f;
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = avg + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
        for (int y = 0; y < w; y += half)             /* square: edge midpoints */
            for (int x = (y / half) % 2 == 0 ? half : 0; x < w; x += step) {
                float sum = 0;
                int cnt = 0;
                if (x >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x - half]; cnt++; }
                if (x + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)y * w + x + half]; cnt++; }
                if (y >= half) { sum += z[(size_t)(y - half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                if (y + half < w) { sum += z[(size_t)(y + half) * w + x]; cnt++; }
                z[(size_t)y * w + x] = sum / cnt + (hash01(x, y, seed + 1) * 2 - 1) * amp;
            }
    }
    return 1;
}

void hm_island(Heightmap *m, float inner, float outer) {
    float sx = 2.0f / (m->w - 1), sy = 2.0f / (m->h - 1);
    for (int y = 0; y < m->h; y++) {
        float ny = y * sy - 1;
        float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * m->w;
        for (int x = 0; x < m->w; x++) {
            float nx = x * sx - 1;
            float d = sqrtf(nx * nx + ny * ny);
            row[x] *= 1 - smoothstepf(inner, outer, d);
        }
    }
}

void hm_terrace(Heightmap *m, int levels, float sharpness) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float t = m->z[i] * levels, k = floorf(t), f = t - k;
        m->z[i] = (k + powf(f, sharpness)) / levels;
    }
}

void hm_thermal(Heightmap *m, int iterations, float talus, float rate) {
    static const int DX[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0}, DY[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    size_t n = (size_t)w * h;
    float *delta = malloc(n * sizeof(float));
    for (int it = 0; it < iterations; it++) {
        memset(delta, 0, n * sizeof(float));
        for (int y = 0; y < h; y++)
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                size_t c = (size_t)y * w + x;
                float hc = m->z[c], d[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0}, dmax = 0, dtot = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    int nx = x + DX[k], ny = y + DY[k];
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
                    d[k] = hc - m->z[(size_t)ny * w + nx];
                    if (d[k] > dmax) dmax = d[k];
                    if (d[k] > talus) dtot += d[k];
                }
                if (dmax <= talus) continue;
                float amount = rate * (dmax - talus);
                for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
                    if (!(d[k] > talus)) continue;
                    float s = amount * d[k] / dtot;
                    delta[(size_t)(y + DY[k]) * w + x + DX[k]] += s;
                    delta[c] -= s;
                }
            }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) m->z[i] += delta[i];
    }
    free(delta);
}

float hm_sample(const Heightmap *m, float x, float y, float *gx, float *gy) {
    int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
    float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy;
    const float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    float nw = p[0], ne = p[1], sw = p[m->w], se = p[m->w + 1];
    if (gx) *gx = (ne - nw) * (1 - fy) + (se - sw) * fy;
    if (gy) *gy = (sw - nw) * (1 - fx) + (se - ne) * fx;
    return nw * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy) + ne * fx * (1 - fy) + sw * (1 - fx) * fy + se * fx * fy;
}

typedef struct {
    float inertia, capacity, min_capacity, deposition, erosion, evaporation, gravity;
    int max_steps;
} ErosionParams;

ErosionParams erosion_defaults(void) {
    ErosionParams p = {0.05f, 4.0f, 0.01f, 0.3f, 0.3f, 0.01f, 4.0f, 30};
    return p;
}

void hm_add_bilinear(Heightmap *m, int ix, int iy, float fx, float fy, float amount) {
    float *p = m->z + (size_t)iy * m->w + ix;
    p[0] += amount * (1 - fx) * (1 - fy);
    p[1] += amount * fx * (1 - fy);
    p[m->w] += amount * (1 - fx) * fy;
    p[m->w + 1] += amount * fx * fy;
}

float erode_droplet(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
            sediment += ero;
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

typedef struct { int n; int *dx, *dy; float *w; } Brush;

Brush brush_new(int radius) {
    size_t cap = (size_t)(2 * radius + 1) * (2 * radius + 1);
    Brush b = {0, malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(int)), malloc(cap * sizeof(float))};
    float sum = 0;
    for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
        for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
            float d = sqrtf((float)(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (d >= radius) continue;
            b.dx[b.n] = dx; b.dy[b.n] = dy; b.w[b.n] = radius - d;
            sum += b.w[b.n++];
        }
    for (int i = 0; i < b.n; i++) b.w[i] /= sum;
    return b;
}

void brush_free(Brush *b) {
    free(b->dx); free(b->dy); free(b->w);
    b->dx = b->dy = NULL; b->w = NULL; b->n = 0;
}

float droplet_run(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, const Brush *brush, float x, float y) {
    if (!(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < m->w - 1 && y < m->h - 1)) return 0;
    float dx = 0, dy = 0, speed = 1, water = 1, sediment = 0;
    for (int step = 0; step < p->max_steps; step++) {
        int ix = (int)x, iy = (int)y;
        float fx = x - ix, fy = y - iy, gx, gy;
        float height = hm_sample(m, x, y, &gx, &gy);
        dx = dx * p->inertia - gx * (1 - p->inertia);
        dy = dy * p->inertia - gy * (1 - p->inertia);
        float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len == 0) break;
        dx /= len; dy /= len;
        x += dx; y += dy;
        if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= m->w - 1 || y >= m->h - 1) break;
        float dh = hm_sample(m, x, y, NULL, NULL) - height;
        float cap = fmaxf(-dh * speed * water * p->capacity, p->min_capacity);
        if (sediment > cap || dh > 0) {
            float dep = dh > 0 ? fminf(dh, sediment) : (sediment - cap) * p->deposition;
            sediment -= dep;
            hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, dep);
        } else {
            float ero = fminf((cap - sediment) * p->erosion, -dh);
            if (!brush) {
                hm_add_bilinear(m, ix, iy, fx, fy, -ero);
                sediment += ero;
            } else {
                for (int k = 0; k < brush->n; k++) {
                    int cx = ix + brush->dx[k], cy = iy + brush->dy[k];
                    if (cx < 0 || cy < 0 || cx >= m->w || cy >= m->h) continue;
                    float *cell = m->z + (size_t)cy * m->w + cx;
                    float want = ero * brush->w[k];
                    float taken = *cell < want ? *cell : want;
                    *cell -= taken;
                    sediment += taken;
                }
            }
        }
        speed = sqrtf(fmaxf(0.0f, speed * speed - dh * p->gravity));
        water *= 1 - p->evaporation;
    }
    return sediment;
}

void hm_erode(Heightmap *m, const ErosionParams *p, uint32_t seed, int droplets, int radius) {
    Brush b = brush_new(radius);
    for (int i = 0; i < droplets; i++)
        droplet_run(m, p, &b, hash01(i, 0, seed) * (m->w - 1), hash01(i, 1, seed) * (m->h - 1));
    brush_free(&b);
}

void hm_normals(const Heightmap *m, float height_scale, float *out) {
    int w = m->w, h = m->h;
    float s = 0.5f * height_scale;
    for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const float *row = m->z + (size_t)y * w;
        const float *up = m->z + (size_t)(y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0) * w;
        const float *dn = m->z + (size_t)(y < h - 1 ? y + 1 : h - 1) * w;
        for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < w - 1 ? x + 1 : w - 1;
            float dx = (row[xr] - row[xl]) * s, dy = (dn[x] - up[x]) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            float *o = out + 3 * ((size_t)y * w + x);
            o[0] = -dx * il; o[1] = -dy * il; o[2] = il;
        }
    }
}

Vec3 light_dir(float azimuth_deg, float altitude_deg) {
    float az = azimuth_deg * 3.14159265f / 180, alt = altitude_deg * 3.14159265f / 180;
    return v3(cosf(alt) * cosf(az), cosf(alt) * sinf(az), sinf(alt));
}

void hm_shade(const Heightmap *m, const float *normals, Vec3 light, float sea, Image *out) {
    size_t n = (size_t)m->w * m->h;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float h = m->z[i], r, g, b, t;
        Vec3 nr = v3(normals[3 * i], normals[3 * i + 1], normals[3 * i + 2]);
        float slope = 1 - nr.z;
        if (h < sea) {
            t = h / sea;
            r = lerpf(20, 40, t); g = lerpf(50, 110, t); b = lerpf(120, 170, t);
            nr = v3(0, 0, 1);
        } else if (h < sea + 0.02f) { r = 200; g = 185; b = 130; }
        else if (slope > 0.3f) { r = 115; g = 105; b = 95; }
        else if (h > 0.8f) { r = 240; g = 240; b = 245; }
        else {
            t = (h - sea) / (0.8f - sea);
            r = lerpf(80, 50, t); g = lerpf(140, 95, t); b = lerpf(60, 40, t);
        }
        float lit = 0.35f + 0.65f * fmaxf(0, v3_dot(nr, light));
        unsigned char *px = out->px + 3 * i;
        px[0] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(r * lit + 0.5f));
        px[1] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(g * lit + 0.5f));
        px[2] = (unsigned char)fminf(255, (int)(b * lit + 0.5f));
    }
}

typedef struct { int nverts, ntris; float *pos, *nrm; uint32_t *idx; } Mesh;

int hm_mesh(const Heightmap *m, int step, float cell, float hscale, Mesh *out) {
    memset(out, 0, sizeof *out);
    if (step < 1 || m->w < 2 || m->h < 2 || (m->w - 1) % step || (m->h - 1) % step) return 0;
    int gw = (m->w - 1) / step + 1, gh = (m->h - 1) / step + 1;
    size_t nv = (size_t)gw * gh, ni = (size_t)6 * (gw - 1) * (gh - 1);
    out->pos = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->nrm = malloc(nv * 3 * sizeof(float));
    out->idx = malloc(ni * sizeof(uint32_t));
    out->nverts = (int)nv;
    out->ntris = (int)(ni / 3);
    float s = 0.5f * hscale / cell;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw; i++) {
            int x = i * step, y = j * step;
            size_t v = (size_t)j * gw + i;
            int xl = x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0, xr = x < m->w - 1 ? x + 1 : m->w - 1;
            int yu = y > 0 ? y - 1 : 0, yd = y < m->h - 1 ? y + 1 : m->h - 1;
            float dx = (hm_get(m, xr, y) - hm_get(m, xl, y)) * s;
            float dy = (hm_get(m, x, yd) - hm_get(m, x, yu)) * s;
            float il = 1.0f / sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + 1);
            out->pos[3 * v] = x * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 1] = y * cell; out->pos[3 * v + 2] = hm_get(m, x, y) * hscale;
            out->nrm[3 * v] = -dx * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 1] = -dy * il; out->nrm[3 * v + 2] = il;
        }
    uint32_t *ix = out->idx;
    for (int j = 0; j < gh - 1; j++)
        for (int i = 0; i < gw - 1; i++) {
            uint32_t a = (uint32_t)(j * gw + i), b = a + 1, c = a + (uint32_t)gw, d = c + 1;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = b; *ix++ = d;
            *ix++ = a; *ix++ = d; *ix++ = c;
        }
    return 1;
}

void mesh_free(Mesh *me) {
    free(me->pos); free(me->nrm); free(me->idx);
    memset(me, 0, sizeof *me);
}

size_t mesh_obj(const Mesh *me, char *buf, size_t cap) {
    size_t len = 0;
    if (buf && cap) buf[0] = '\0';
#define OBJ_PUT(...) do { \
        int k_ = snprintf(len < cap ? buf + len : NULL, len < cap ? cap - len : 0, __VA_ARGS__); \
        if (k_ > 0) len += (size_t)k_; \
    } while (0)
    for (int i = 0; i < me->nverts; i++)
        OBJ_PUT("v %.3f %.3f %.3f\n", me->pos[3 * i], me->pos[3 * i + 1], me->pos[3 * i + 2]);
    for (int i = 0; i < me->nverts; i++)
        OBJ_PUT("vn %.3f %.3f %.3f\n", me->nrm[3 * i], me->nrm[3 * i + 1], me->nrm[3 * i + 2]);
    for (int t = 0; t < me->ntris; t++) {
        unsigned a = me->idx[3 * t] + 1, b = me->idx[3 * t + 1] + 1, c = me->idx[3 * t + 2] + 1;
        OBJ_PUT("f %u//%u %u//%u %u//%u\n", a, a, b, b, c, c);
    }
#undef OBJ_PUT
    return len;
}

typedef struct { float x, y, height, angle, horizon, scale, distance; } VoxelCam;

void hm_render(const Heightmap *m, float hscale, const Image *colors, const VoxelCam *cam, Image *out) {
    int W = out->w, H = out->h;
    for (int i = 0; i < W * H; i++) { out->px[3 * i] = 150; out->px[3 * i + 1] = 190; out->px[3 * i + 2] = 230; }
    int *ybuf = malloc(sizeof(int) * W);
    for (int i = 0; i < W; i++) ybuf[i] = H;
    float ca = cosf(cam->angle), sa = sinf(cam->angle);
    float z = 1, dz = 1;
    while (z < cam->distance) {
        float plx = cam->x + z * (ca - sa), ply = cam->y + z * (sa + ca);
        float prx = cam->x + z * (ca + sa), pry = cam->y + z * (sa - ca);
        float ddx = (prx - plx) / W, ddy = (pry - ply) / W;
        for (int sx = 0; sx < W; sx++, plx += ddx, ply += ddy) {
            int ix = (int)floorf(plx), iy = (int)floorf(ply);
            if (ix < 0 || iy < 0 || ix >= m->w || iy >= m->h) continue;
            int row = (int)((cam->height - hm_get(m, ix, iy) * hscale) / z * cam->scale + cam->horizon);
            if (row < 0) row = 0;
            if (row >= ybuf[sx]) continue;
            const unsigned char *c = colors->px + 3 * ((size_t)iy * colors->w + ix);
            for (int r = row; r < ybuf[sx]; r++) {
                unsigned char *o = out->px + 3 * ((size_t)r * W + sx);
                o[0] = c[0]; o[1] = c[1]; o[2] = c[2];
            }
            ybuf[sx] = row;
        }
        z += dz;
        dz += 0.02f;
    }
    free(ybuf);
}

int main(void) {
    Heightmap m = hm_new(256, 256);
    hm_fbm(&m, 7, 1.0f / 64, 7);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    hm_island(&m, 0.2f, 0.95f);
    ErosionParams p = erosion_defaults();
    hm_erode(&m, &p, 1, 40000, 3);
    hm_normalize(&m);
    float *n = malloc(sizeof(float) * 3 * 256 * 256);
    hm_normals(&m, 25, n);
    Image colors = img_new(256, 256);
    hm_shade(&m, n, light_dir(225, 40), 0.3f, &colors);
    for (int i = 0; i < 256 * 256; i++) if (m.z[i] < 0.3f) m.z[i] = 0.3f;   /* flat sea surface */
    VoxelCam cam = {128, 250, 70, -1.5707963f, 30, 60, 400};
    Image out = img_new(128, 96);
    hm_render(&m, 60, &colors, &cam, &out);
    img_write_ppm(&out);
    img_free(&out); img_free(&colors); free(n); hm_free(&m);
    return 0;
}

--- hint
Accumulate `pl += d` per column rather than recomputing `pl + d * sx`; that is what the reference does, and it avoids a multiply per column.

--- hint
Painting goes from the new (higher, smaller) row down to the old y-buffer value, exclusive.

--- harness
static void ref_render(const Heightmap *m, float hs, const Image *col, const VoxelCam *c, Image *out) {
    int W = out->w, H = out->h, yb[512];
    for (int i = 0; i < W * H; i++) { out->px[3 * i] = 150; out->px[3 * i + 1] = 190; out->px[3 * i + 2] = 230; }
    for (int i = 0; i < W; i++) yb[i] = H;
    float ca = cosf(c->angle), sa = sinf(c->angle), z = 1, dz = 1;
    while (z < c->distance) {
        float lx = c->x + z * (ca - sa), ly = c->y + z * (sa + ca), rx = c->x + z * (ca + sa), ry = c->y + z * (sa - ca);
        float dx = (rx - lx) / W, dy = (ry - ly) / W;
        for (int s = 0; s < W; s++, lx += dx, ly += dy) {
            int ix = (int)floorf(lx), iy = (int)floorf(ly);
            if (ix < 0 || iy < 0 || ix >= m->w || iy >= m->h) continue;
            int row = (int)((c->height - m->z[iy * m->w + ix] * hs) / z * c->scale + c->horizon);
            if (row < 0) row = 0;
            for (int r = row; r < yb[s]; r++) memcpy(out->px + 3 * (r * W + s), col->px + 3 * (iy * col->w + ix), 3);
            if (row < yb[s]) yb[s] = row;
        }
        z += dz; dz += 0.02f;
    }
}
static int same_px(const Image *a, const Image *b) {
    int n = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < a->w * a->h; i++) n += memcmp(a->px + 3 * i, b->px + 3 * i, 3) == 0;
    return n;
}
int main(void) {
    /* flat ground at height 0, camera 10 up, looking +x: horizon row at 20, ground color below it */
    Heightmap f = hm_new(200, 200);
    Image fc = img_new(200, 200);
    for (int i = 0; i < 200 * 200; i++) { fc.px[3 * i] = 10; fc.px[3 * i + 1] = 200; fc.px[3 * i + 2] = 20; }
    VoxelCam c = {100, 100, 10, 0, 20, 30, 150};
    Image o = img_new(40, 40);
    hm_render(&f, 1, &fc, &c, &o);
    CHECK(o.px[3 * (5 * 40 + 20)] == 150 && o.px[3 * (5 * 40 + 20) + 2] == 230);
    CHECK(o.px[3 * (39 * 40 + 20)] == 10 && o.px[3 * (39 * 40 + 20) + 1] == 200);
    CHECK(o.px[3 * (24 * 40 + 3)] == 10);
    CHECK(o.px[3 * (19 * 40 + 20)] == 150);
    Image r = img_new(40, 40);
    ref_render(&f, 1, &fc, &c, &r);
    CHECK_INT(same_px(&o, &r), 40 * 40);

    /* a red wall at x = 130 hides what is behind it */
